# Third-party notices

Nodearr is free software under the GNU General Public License v3.0 (`LICENSE`). Most of it is derived from Sonarr and Prowlarr (`NOTICE.md`). A release also carries the work of the people and projects below, under their own licences, whose texts are given here as those licences ask.

This file is generated by `build/Build-Notices.ps1` from what a release is built from. Do not edit it by hand.

The self-contained packages also carry the .NET runtime, which is under the MIT licence with notices of its own. Those are in each package as `dotnet-LICENSE.txt` and `dotnet-THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt`. The Docker image is built on Microsoft's `aspnet` image, which carries them itself.

Source code: Nodearr publishes everything here unmodified, and its source is where each entry links to. That is where the source of the packages under the Mozilla Public License 2.0 (`attohttpc` in the sidecar, `IPAddressRange` in the application) is available, as section 3.2 of that licence asks. `ffprobe` is under the GNU Lesser General Public License, and its entry says where its corresponding source is.

## The transport sidecar, `nodearr-transport`

Built from the Rust crates below. Each licence's text follows the list of crates under it.

- Apache License 2.0: 216 crate(s)
- MIT License: 72 crate(s)
- Unicode License v3: 19 crate(s)
- ISC License: 18 crate(s)
- BSD 3-Clause "New" or "Revised" License: 5 crate(s)
- BSD 2-Clause "Simplified" License: 1 crate(s)
- Community Data License Agreement Permissive 2.0: 1 crate(s)
- Mozilla Public License 2.0: 1 crate(s)
- zlib License: 1 crate(s)

#ifndef OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H
#define OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

#include "../../limbs/limbs.h"

#define P384_LIMBS (384u / LIMB_BITS)

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Z[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT;

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT_AFFINE;


#endif // OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

// SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF OR IN
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

//! EdDSA Signatures.

use super::ops::ELEM_LEN;
use crate::digest;

pub mod signing;
pub mod verification;

/// The length of an Ed25519 public key.
pub const ED25519_PUBLIC_KEY_LEN: usize = ELEM_LEN;

pub fn eddsa_digest(signature_r: &[u8], public_key: &[u8], msg: &[u8]) -> digest::Digest {
    let mut ctx = digest::Context::new(&digest::SHA512);
    ctx.update(signature_r);
    ctx.update(public_key);
    ctx.update(msg);
    ctx.finish()
}

```

// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF OR IN
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

use crate::limb::Limb;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct N0([Limb; 2]);

impl N0 {
    #[cfg(feature = "alloc")]
    pub(super) const LIMBS_USED: usize = 64 / crate::limb::LIMB_BITS;

    #[inline]
    pub const fn precalculated(n0: u64) -> Self {
        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
        {
            Self([n0, 0])
        }

        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "32")]
        {
            Self([n0 as Limb, (n0 >> crate::limb::LIMB_BITS) as Limb])
        }
    }
}

```

use crate::error::{KeyRejected, Unspecified};

impl From<untrusted::EndOfInput> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: untrusted::EndOfInput) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<core::array::TryFromSliceError> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: core::array::TryFromSliceError) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<KeyRejected> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: KeyRejected) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

### [Openur.FFprobeStatic 8.1.1.343](https://www.nuget.org/packages/Openur.FFprobeStatic/8.1.1.343)

Openur Team

The package carries ffprobe, from FFmpeg 8.1.1, built statically for each platform by the Openur
team with two patches of theirs. It is configured with --enable-version3 and without
--enable-gpl, so this build of FFmpeg is under the GNU Lesser General Public License, version 3
or later. Its text is under "The LGPL-3.0-or-later licence" in this part. It also contains dav1d,
under the BSD 2-Clause licence, whose text is under "The BSD-2-Clause licence".

Corresponding source:

- FFmpeg 8.1.1: https://ffmpeg.org/releases/ (ffmpeg-8.1.1.tar.xz)
- The build scripts, configure flags and patches, at the commit this package was built from:
  https://github.com/Openur/ffprobe-static-build/tree/9e8070ec8ea989667b2b24be532c317b9c27c33d
- dav1d 1.5.3: https://code.videolan.org/videolan/dav1d/-/releases

Nodearr runs ffprobe as a separate program and does not modify it.
